HhobohunterApr 10 2011, 09:26 PM
Are you talking about the Psycho Cyan Glitch? Where Cyan uses Sky after getting revived while he has the Imp status and was KO'd. This causes Cyan to retaliate with a normal attack instead of Sky causing an infinite loop of counterattacks because Sky is supposed to counterattack at normal attacks. the glitch causes the Sky status to not disappear while he is imp'd.

TThe Lone WandererApr 11 2011, 02:40 AM
If i recall correctly using vanish on the enemies causes magic spells to have a 100% hit rate on them. Using X-zone or doom would instantly kill any enemies or even bosses with this trick.

Ppan_dim_oniumApr 15 2011, 12:31 AM
Okay, well my intention was the first -new- set of jobs as in after your initial set of jobs. So not White Mage. You start out as an onion knight. one of the other jobs has a menu ability for scanning. which job?
TThe Lone WandererApr 15 2011, 01:20 AM
I believe he's referring the the **** useless scholar class. It only knows Peep which tells you the enemy's hp and scan which tells you their weakness. Technically it can't use magic unless you count those two skills as magic. The gunblade uses shotgun rounds oddly enough. It can only be obtained by getting an S rank after beating the game and buying it in the shop. There was also a rumor that you can get the gunblade from No.9 in Dryfield if you have the medicine wheel equipped though I've never seen if it was true or not.

TThe Lone WandererApr 16 2011, 01:02 AM
Lets see now, those little red balls are called movers. I guess they call them movers because as you destroy them they switch formations and move around. They were useful to fight because of the tremendous AP they give you. Front mission refers to their mechs as Wanzers. I guess the biggest difference between 1 and 2 is that in 2 your mission objective can change to something else in the middle of battle. I don't believe 1 did that.

KKibaApr 16 2011, 10:47 PM
There are two possible reasons that the vanish x-zone trick works. The first reason is that buffs such as vanish have a 100% hit rate. Once vanish has been placed on someone the game uses the mdef. stat to figure out effectiveness of spells. The game doesn't really make magic hit 100%. When the game looks at the mdef. stat it sees it as 0 because they didn't properly code the game to read the data. The only defense stat that actually matters in FFVI is the pdef.The second reason is similar to the first the difference being that because the game doesn't know how to check the mdef. stat it makes the acc. 100% because it gets confused and has to fix the logical conundrum.

Kiba seemed to understand it very well, that the game checks against one thing, then applies that blindly without checking for immunity and such. Final Fantasy games, for some reason, often have stats problems (I remember hearing that the MDef stat in FF7 was useless, or something) so he gets the points. What I really was looking for was kind of an order of operations type explanation. Both from IX. Heat KO's you after making an action. Freeze the character is incased in Ice and is KO'd if he/she is hit with a physical attack.

EXTRA QUESTIONS: EMPTYQUESTIONS:FINAL FANTASY QUESTION:Q28: In Final Fantasy 12, what makes the process for acquiring the Zodiac Spear so special? (1 point)NOTE: Explain what odd circumstances must be followed to be able to get this weapon.SQUARE ENIX QUESTION:Q23: Let's go for a quick foot race on the beach! But wait, which game would we need for that? (1 point)HINT: Not the tutorial for Kingdom Hearts. It's a mini-game.
TThe Lone WandererApr 23 2011, 04:00 AM
Q.28: Ugh the Zodiac Spear. I remember not even knowing about it my first run. Basically in order to even get the weapon there are some treasure chests in 4 different parts of the game that you aren't supposed to collect. If you've done that then you will find it in the Necrohol of Nabudis. However you can obtain a Zodiac Spear from one of the chests in the Henne Mines. The odds are like 1/1000 though I think.
BBash the GovernmentApr 23 2011, 07:12 AM
Vibra Prime' post='4946583 wrote on Apr 23 2011, 02:00 PM:
Q.28: Ugh the Zodiac Spear. I remember not even knowing about it my first run. Basically in order to even get the weapon there are some treasure chests in 4 different parts of the game that you aren't supposed to collect. If you've done that then you will find it in the Necrohol of Nabudis. However you can obtain a Zodiac Spear from one of the chests in the Henne Mines. The odds are like 1/1000 though I think.
Can I elaborate, just for the sake of being nerdy.- The Zodiac Spear in the Necrohol of Nabudis, requires you to avoid opening the treasures found:1. Outside Old Dalan's House2. Southwest Corner of treasures found in the Royal Palace.3. The treasures in the Nalbina Dungeon repository.4. The sixteen treasures in a 4x4 at the Phon Coast.Kinda seems weird, but more so that someone found it.- The Zodiac Spear in the Henne Mines is located in an uncharted zone (I'm taking a guess and saying it is somewhere after the Zodiark arena.You can't be wearing the Diamond Armlet (or whatever gives superior treasures) accessory, as it will knock out the 1/1000 chance to get it.You could probably use the float trap trick to force it though.

+2 for Kiba and Vibra Prime.Of the four spells unique to Selphie that made it into the game (Full Cure, Barrier, The End, and Rapture) only The End and Rapture might fit this question. Q79: ok correct me if i'm wrong cause i experienced this glitch a long time ago. Basically what happens is you go to a pachisi track. From there you proceed to go to a stat affecting square. For me i lost 1 str so i'm not too sure if it works with other stats as well. After you have lost your stat, the next time you level up, you will shoot all the way to lvl 99.Q77.
